Chromium-only scratch copies were run instead and all pass; Safari/iOS-specific rendering (the owner's iPhone preview scenario) remains unverified here.; tests/demo/legacy/b1_test.py: skipped on purpose — it is a live-stack test that signs up users and inserts into drugbox_live.auth.users; the e2e baseline agent covers tests/e2e/b1_auth_test.py.; The stress 'before' baseline: /tmp/backup/drugbox_before_batch4.html does not exist in the repo; it was pointed at web/base/app.html (approved demo without any Drugbox layer), so the comparison measures the whole layer stack, not the last feature.; The one-off 'insertAdjacentHTML … has no parent' page error (1 in 1,500 switches) was not reproduced deterministically; attribution to videos.js:79 is by code reading.; Timing numbers (stress/perf/xbrowser) were taken on a shared 4-CPU machine with other agents running; absolute values carry noise, the base-vs-current ratio was consistent across two paired runs.

### db-0007-0010
Read all four migrations plus their dependencies (0001 is_company_member/companies/approved_suppliers/jobs/job_applications/enquiries, 0006 companies guard, 0015 counter fix) and the live adapter's use of them (web/src/live/adapter.js, web/src/deals.js, web/src/directory3.js). Built a private scratch database rv_db0007 from _local_supabase_stub.sql + migrations 0001–0019 and executed every scenario as ordinary users (set_config request.jwt.claims + set role authenticated/anon), including three concurrency races with parallel psql sessions, a 3,000-company / 60k-deal / 240k-event load for directory_companies(), and read-only HTTP checks against the shared PostgREST (:3001) and gateway (:54321) as anon. Verdict: the state machine itself is solid (no illegal transition, no double act, FOR UPDATE serialises races correctly, direct table writes are blocked), but authorisation around the "from" side is wrong (an ex-member keeps full control; any role writes the AVL through the engine), the public track record is trivially self-inflatable, group buying accepts NaN/Infinity/absurd quantities that close or brick any public group, anon can read every buying group, and embedding the track record in directory_companies() makes the directory ~9x slower. Scratch DB dropped at the end.

**اتفحص وسليم:** State machine: every action in deal_flow has a deal_next_status mapping; out-of-turn, wrong-side and outsider actions are refused (deals.rls.sql + my scratch runs); job/dossier/service/surplus paths follow the demo's FLOWS table exactly.; Idempotency/races: deal_act and deal_join take `select … for update`; parallel psql sessions — accept vs revise on the same quote, two joins at the brink of the target, two confirm_group calls — each produced exactly one winner, the loser was refused after the lock released, one 'target' event and one order per member (2), no duplicates.; Direct writes: insert/update/delete on deals, deal_events, deal_members are 'permission denied' for authenticated and anon; changes only via the three functions; service-only behaviour preserved.; Rating input: stars outside 1–5, '0', floats ('5.5') refused; rating twice refused (status closed); stars smuggled in p_data of other actions are ignored by company_track_record (filters action='rate').; Expired offers refused on accept (deals.rls.sql); counter → new quote → accept path works.; Group buying basics: zero/half-unit shares refused, supplier cannot join its own group, join after target_reached refused, confirm before target refused, 'by' date enforced, double confirmation impossible (confirmed state has no acts).; Questionnaire approval upserts approved_suppliers with the 0008 status list; outsiders cannot read another company's AVL.; company_listings RLS: only owner/admin/sales/regulatory of the company can insert and created_by must be the caller; moving a listing to another company refused; off outside 0–95 refused; inactive listings visible only to the team.; Enquiries: default id 'E'+10 hex generated on insert without id; client-chosen id allowed but duplicates refused; own-manage policy prevents inserting for other users.; Jobs: category check constraint enforced ('marketing' refused, adapter's catOf only emits allowed values); applicant_count stays equal to real count across apply → withdraw → re-apply; duplicate apply refused by PK and upsert does not double count; after 0015 exactly one counter trigger (trg_applicant_count) remains — schema_sweep.sql prints 'none' for all three checks on the full 0001–0019 build.; Refs: demo ids are 'D'+7 random base36 chars (unguessable), so client-chosen p_ref/listing ref squatting is impractical; ref lookup in deal_act is security definer but outsiders are refused.

**ما اتغطاش:** Browser-level checks of the deals/groups UI (Playwright) were not run; the HTTP checks used anon only because the shared stack's JWT secret is random per start and I could not mint a user token without touching the shared gateway.; Real Supabase default privileges were inferred from the stub (grant all on tables to anon/authenticated); exact production grants should be confirmed on the real project.; k6-scale load (100k users / 2,000 concurrent) was not simulated; the directory timing is a single-session measurement on the 4-CPU box.

### db-0011-0017
Read all seven migrations plus the 0001/0004/0006/0018 definitions they depend on, the local Supabase stub, the gateway's storage emulation and the live adapter's upload/sign/moderation code. Built a scratch database rv_db0011 from the stub + migrations 0001–0019 (schema_sweep: three "none" lines) and ran ~90 statements as six simulated users (normal users A/B, platform admin, moderator, company owner, non-admin team member) and anon against storage.objects rows and the public tables; the scratch DB was dropped afterwards. Storage policies themselves hold up well: every cross-folder write, rename, read and delete I tried was denied, documents are readable only by the right parties, message files only by the two people, and signed links are only issued when the select policy passes. The serious problems are around them: (1) CRITICAL — any user can UPDATE their own profiles.role to 'admin' (no with-check/column guard), which turns every is_platform_admin() gate in 0016/0017/0018/companies_guard into a self-service switch (approve own verification, read all verification and payment documents, close reports, moderate warnings, set VIP plan); (2) the reported company can dismiss and rewrite "wrong information" reports before Drugbox sees them; (3) a single unsolicited message counts as a "real interaction", so anyone can leave an anonymous review shown as "Verified applicant"; (4) private groups are a dead end (invisible and unjoinable) and the live adapter silently creates them as public; (5) deleting a post leaves its public files in the post-media bucket forever. Several lower items (group admins rewriting membership rows, reference-evidence bucket without limits, unvalidated path columns, moderators seeing empty review queues) are listed with evidence.

**اتفحص وسليم:** videos (0011): upload/rename/delete only under people/<own uid>/ or companies/<id>/ as owner/admin — writes to another user's folder, uppercase uuid, unknown prefix, single-segment name, non-member company folder, sales-role member, bigint overflow id and renaming an own object into someone else's folder were all denied; `..` segments are stored as a literal key (no overwrite of another key; browsers normalize the public URL) and Node's URL parser normalizes `..`/%2e%2e before the local gateway touches the filesystem; message-media (0014): sender may only upload under <own uid>/…; receiver reads and cannot delete; admin, third party and anon read 0 rows; messages_attachment_path CHECK rejects an attachment outside sender/receiver/; signed links are issued only when the select policy passes (gateway sign endpoint runs the select as the user, Supabase does the same) and carry an HMAC token, so a third party cannot guess or enumerate usable links; messages cannot be deleted or edited (guard trigger covers attachment); documents (0016/0018): cv/<uid>/ readable by the applicant and only by the poster of the job that application carries (exact cv_path match); verification/<id>/ by company owner/admin and platform admin only (sales-role member denied, moderator denied); payments/<uid>/ by the user and admin; cross-folder writes into another user's cv/ or verification/ denied; cv_path CHECK stops attaching another applicant's CV to one's own application; no update policy so no overwrite/upsert of existing objects; post-media (0014): insert only under posts/<own uid>/, no update policy, delete own only; post_media rows readable by all but insertable only by the post's author (B attaching to A's post denied); empty-body posts allowed as documented; group_members (0013): self-join only, only public groups, role forced to 'member' (self-promotion denied), private-group members invisible to non-members, creator auto-admin, single counter trigger with recount (member_count correct after join/leave), creator cannot hand the group to someone else (own-manage USING doubles as WITH CHECK); company_followers (0015): follow/unfollow as self only, own follows visible only to self, primary key prevents double follow, exactly one counter trigger (trg_follower_count) and follower_count goes 0→1→0 correctly; notifications / decisions (0017): a user cannot insert a notification for another user; verification_requests status can only be changed by a platform admin (owner and moderator denied; owner cannot even edit note); approve/reject produce the expected notifications with from_user = reviewing admin and company becomes verified/tax_verified; work_references status cannot be set by its author (no update policy), moderate_reference refuses non-moderators, publishing a warning notifies the candidate; unverified users do not see published warnings; get_reviews_many (0012): anonymous reviews return author NULL (get_reviews returns the label), hidden reviews excluded, `mine` only true for the author, raw job_reviews rows invisible to the reviewee; my_interactions restricted to authenticated and returns only the caller's own counterparties; open_candidates excludes the caller; schema_sweep after 0001–0019 on a fresh database: 'RLS on, no policy: none', 'RLS off: none', 'Tables with two counters: none'; 0015 correctly removes the duplicate applicant counter from 0010

**ما اتغطاش:** Real Supabase Storage API behaviour (object-key validation, upsert = insert+update semantics, signed-URL JWT format, 50 MB global upload cap) was reasoned from the platform's documented behaviour and the fetched foldername source, not executed against a Supabase project — no project credentials exist here; The private-group selector mismatch was established from the approved markup and the adapter selector, not by driving the create-group modal in a browser against the shared drugbox_live stack (avoided writing rows into the shared database); Realtime publication of notifications and the Admin → Review page rendering for a moderator account were not exercised in a browser; Performance of group_member_count's count(*) recount on very large groups and of get_reviews_many with very large p_ids arrays was not load-tested (k6 is a later step per CLAUDE.md)

### db-0018-0019-payments
Read the whole money path and TESTED it two ways: (a) a scratch DB (rv_dbpay) built from the stub + all 0001-0019 migrations, driving create_order/confirm_payment/activate_order/submit_instapay/review_instapay as anon/authenticated/service_role with request.jwt.claims, and (b) an isolated HTTP rig running the REAL function code under Deno (functions-local.js) against my own PostgREST + proxy + the mock providers on private ports, forging/replaying Paymob (HMAC-SHA512) and Fawry (SHA-256 V2) callbacks with signatures computed independently. The core server-side controls are SOLID: confirm_payment/activate_order/set_order_provider_ref are security definer AND revoked from public/anon/authenticated (verified with has_function_privilege); prices/VAT come only from payment_products (browser cannot set amount/status); direct writes to payment_orders are blocked by RLS; forged/empty/upper-hex-case HMACs are rejected; underpayment is rejected for both providers; replay of a callback for the same order is idempotent ('already paid'); orders are per-user; InstaPay approval is admin-only with ownership + receipt-folder checks; training RLS blocks enrolling others, adding courses, reading others' enrollments and tampering the counter; schema sweep prints three 'none' lines and there is one counter trigger. The real problems are in what happens AFTER a correct payment: paid Boost/Featured has no effect in the live app (the *_until columns are written but never read), VIP never expires (vip_until is never enforced and no cron downgrades), activate_order reports success and charges even when the target listing/company was deleted, and the Paymob order selection relies on a field (merchant_order_id) that is outside the HMAC-signed set with no provider_ref uniqueness. Verdict: no privilege-escalation or free-activation holes, but several money-correctness and post-payment defects that will cost the owner revenue and trust at launch.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Function privileges verified with has_function_privilege in rv_dbpay: confirm_payment, activate_order, set_order_provider_ref are SECURITY DEFINER and have anon=f authn=f public_grant=f, service_role=t (correctly locked); create_order/submit_instapay/review_instapay keep the default PUBLIC grant but each enforces auth.uid()/ownership/is_platform_admin internally, so no escalation.; Prices and VAT come only from payment_products: create_order computes amount_cents = round(amount_egp*(1+vat_rate)*100) server-side; the browser cannot set amount/currency/status. VAT math exact: boost 1450->165300, featured 3950->450300, vip_month 2500->285000, vip_year 25000->2850000 cents (integer, no piastre rounding error).; Direct tampering blocked by RLS: authenticated INSERT into payment_orders and UPDATE of status, and UPDATE of payment_products.amount_egp, all rejected (new row violates RLS / only admin updates prices). payment_orders SELECT is per-user (other user sees 0 rows; anon sees none).; Paymob webhook authenticity: forged (hmac='f'*128), empty, and upper-hex-case signatures all rejected 401 with nothing activated; a signature computed independently from Paymob's 20-field formula is accepted; timing-safe comparison (safeEqual) with length check present.; Fawry webhook authenticity: forged messageSignature and missing-signature rejected 401; independently computed SHA-256 V2 (fawryRefNumber+merchantRefNumber+paymentAmount.00+orderAmount.00+orderStatus+paymentMethod+paymentRefrenceNumber+secureKey) accepted; non-PAID statuses noted without activation.; Idempotency / replay for a single order: replaying the same valid callback returns 'already paid' and does NOT extend vip_until/boosted_until a second time (confirm_payment: if status='paid' return early; FOR UPDATE row lock present).; Underpayment rejected for both providers: a signed Paymob callback with amount_cents=100 and a signed Fawry PAID with orderAmount=1.0 both returned 'amount mismatch' and left status='pending' (confirm_payment amount_cents equality check).; create_order authorization: anon -> 'Sign in first'; a non-owner buying VIP -> 'Only the company's owner or admins can upgrade it'; a non-owner boosting someone else's listing -> 'You can boost only your own listings'; invalid method rejected by CHECK constraint; passing both company_id and listing_id stores only the field matching the product kind (no cross-wiring).; InstaPay flow: submit_instapay enforces user_id=auth.uid(), method='instapay', status='pending', and receipt path prefix 'payments/<uid>/'; a .. traversal path stays a literal storage key (can_read_document keys off folder[1]/[2]='payments/<uid>', so no object-store traversal); transfer_ref truncated to 64 chars; a second submit on a non-pending order is refused; non-admin review_instapay -> 'Drugbox team only'; admin approve activates + featured_until set; re-review of a non-review order -> 'Not waiting for review'.; payments-create input validation: missing JWT->401, bad JSON->400, missing/invalid method->400, unknown product->400, GET->405, OPTIONS->CORS preflight; redirection_url and notification_url are server-built from APP_URL/FUNCTIONS_URL (no user-controlled open redirect); the DB (not the function) is the auth gate via auth.uid().; Secrets handling in the real functions: no console.log of secrets or full payloads; provider_payload stores only {provider,id,order,source_data.type} — no card PAN; secrets read via env only.; Training (0019): schema sweep clean (RLS on/off 'none', two-counter 'none'); exactly one counter trigger trg_course_enrolled_count (+1 insert / -1 delete, floored at 0, accurate across cascade delete of a user); six courses seeded with unique titles; members cannot enroll others, cannot add courses, cannot read others' enrollments, cannot tamper enrolled_count (RLS 0 rows), and cannot enroll in an inactive course (insert policy requires c.active) nor see it.; schema_sweep.sql on the full rv_dbpay (stub + 0001-0019) prints: RLS on,no policy: none / RLS off: none / Tables with two counters: none.

**ما اتغطاش:** Did not drive the browser checkout UI (e4b) or the full shared e2e suite: the shared live gateway on :54321 was already crashed before I began (a pre-existing ERR_OUT_OF_RANGE in tools/local-supabase/gateway.mjs's Range-request handler around line 65 — outside my assigned files), and the task forbids restarting the shared stack. I instead exercised the REAL function code via an isolated Deno rig (functions-local.js) on private ports against my own PostgREST+proxy and the mock providers, which covers the server logic end-to-end.; Did not validate the signature formulas against Paymob's / Fawry's CURRENT production documentation byte-for-byte — I used the formulas the code itself documents (and the mock enforces), and confirmed the independently-computed signatures are accepted/rejected as expected. The merchant_order_id-not-in-HMAC property is consistent with Paymob's documented 20-field HMAC.; Real provider behaviour (partial captures, refunds webhooks, 3DS failures, Fawry EXPIRED notifications) was only reasoned about from the code: confirm_payment has no refund/expire path and payment_orders 'refunded'/'expired' states are never set by any function — flagged indirectly via the deleted-target finding; a dedicated refund/expiry webhook path is absent but may be intended for later.; Did not load-test under concurrency: the confirm_payment FOR UPDATE row lock should serialize webhook-vs-webhook, but a webhook-vs-polling race (adapter.js:1199 polls payment_orders.status) was only read, not stress-tested.

### rls-crosscut
Built a scratch DB (rv_rls_crosscut) from supabase/tests/_local_supabase_stub.sql plus all 19 migrations (clean load; schema_sweep.sql prints the three 'none' lines). Ran catalog queries over all 42 tables, 46 custom SECURITY DEFINER functions, 1 view, 25 sequences, 27 triggers and 74 foreign keys, then tested each suspicious path as the real API roles (set role authenticated/anon with request.jwt.claim.sub inside rolled-back transactions) and once over HTTP through a private PostgREST on the scratch DB. Seeded only through the app's own paths (RLS inserts, deal_create/deal_join/create_order/submit_instapay, the auth.users trigger) and enumerated every table as anon. Verdict: the skeleton is sound (RLS on every table with a policy, definer functions pinned to search_path=public, deal and payment engines server-only, document file paths column-revoked), but row policies are not column policies: a signed-in user can PATCH their own profiles.role to admin (confirmed over HTTP) and from there approve verifications, mark InstaPay orders paid, reprice products and read every private table; listing owners can self-grant boosted/featured; a connection addressee can rewrite the requester; applicants set their own hired status. Three NO ACTION foreign keys make post deletion fail after the first like or comment and make deleting a user impossible once they ever reacted or created a group. 34 FKs have no index (one post delete is about 100 ms at 400k notifications, growing linearly). Scratch DB dropped, no scratch processes left (PID 16931 belongs to another agent's rv_pgrst.conf and was not touched).

**اتفحص وسليم:** schema_sweep.sql after all 19 migrations: 'RLS on, no policy: none', 'RLS off: none', 'Tables with two counters: none'; every public table has RLS and at least one policy; All 46 custom SECURITY DEFINER functions except handle_new_user carry SET search_path=public; trigger functions (returns trigger) cannot be invoked directly via RPC; confirm_payment, activate_order, set_order_provider_ref: EXECUTE revoked from public/anon/authenticated and still granted to service_role (webhooks keep working); create_order: refuses anon ('Sign in first'), takes price from payment_products, checks listing ownership for boost/featured and owner/admin for VIP, amount computed server-side; payment_orders.method has a check constraint (method='free' refused); submit_instapay enforces own order, method=instapay, status=pending and receipt under payments/<uid>/; review_instapay and moderate_reference enforce admin/moderator; reply_to_reference restricted to the candidate; company_documents.file_path: column-level SELECT revoked for anon/authenticated (only id, company_id, type, product, number, expiry, status, created_by, created_at readable); company_documents_public view is security_invoker; outsider gets insufficient_privilege on file_path; companies_guard: non-admin INSERT forced to owner_id=auth.uid(), status=pending, plan=free, verified=false, tax_verified=false, licensed=false; status/plan/vip_until/verified/owner_id changes refused; a platform admin cannot edit another company's row through RLS (only via verification_requests_apply); company_members_guard: only owners add owners/admins, members cannot change their own role, consent columns (accepted/show_public) only by the person; outsiders cannot insert themselves (tested in company_hub.rls.sql and re-verified); messages: readable only by sender/receiver, messages_guard blocks editing body/attachment/parties, attachment path constrained to <sender>/<receiver>/, conversation_heads SELECT-only and populated by definer triggers; deals/deal_events/deal_members: INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE revoked from anon/authenticated; deal_create/deal_act/deal_join require auth and company membership; non-group deals invisible to outsiders and to anon (anon enumeration saw only the group deal); job_applications.cv_path constrained to cv/<user_id>/ and can_read_document lets the job poster read exactly the CV attached to an application to their job; verification/ and payments/ folders gated by company owner/admin or platform admin; storage.objects policies: documents/message-media/reference-evidence are private with path-based checks; uploads require auth (anon's auth.uid() is null); videos and post-media public by design; payment_orders: no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy so direct writes are denied; merchant_ref is a generated column; confirm_payment is idempotent on 'paid' and checks amount; anon/authenticated/service_role have no CREATE privilege on schema public (search_path injection not possible today); Every uuid column named like an actor/owner has a foreign key (no dangling reference columns); all FKs to profiles except groups.created_by, notifications.from_user, verification_requests.reviewed_by are CASCADE or SET NULL; As anon, zero rows readable from messages, notifications, connections, conversation_heads, payment_orders, verification_requests, company_routing, company_reports, job_applications, job_lists, job_reviews (table), saved_posts, saved_jobs, course_enrollments, approved_suppliers, private groups, inactive jobs/products/listings; Only one view exists (company_documents_public) and it is security_invoker=true; Counter triggers are AFTER ROW and SECURITY DEFINER; guard triggers (companies, company_members, site_certificates, company_documents, messages, job_lists, verification_requests) are BEFORE ROW and cannot be bypassed by RLS

**ما اتغطاش:** Not run against a real Supabase project: used the repo's stub (auth.uid/role, storage tables, default privileges) which mirrors Supabase defaults; Supabase's storage API server-side checks and the real auth schema were not exercised; Edge functions (payments-create, paymob-webhook, fawry-webhook) and provider HMAC/signature paths were not executed; only the SQL functions they call were reviewed and tested; Storage policies were verified by reading policy text and testing the helper functions with SQL, not by uploading/downloading real objects through the gateway; No Playwright run of the live UI for these findings; adapter behaviour (post delete toast at web/src/live/adapter.js:208, no consumer of boosted_until) inferred from code grep; Performance figures come from 400k-row tables on the shared 4-CPU box, not from a 100k-user k6 load test; Group-admin force-join via rewriting group_members.user_id was attempted but the check ran in the same statement as the update (stale snapshot) so it is inconclusive and not reported

### adapter-1
Read adapter.js 1-430 line by line against the approved demo (web/base/app.html / web/dist/drugbox.html) and the real schema (live DB drugbox_live + scratch DB rv_adapter1 built from stub + migrations 0001-0019). Every .from()/.rpc() name, column, FK-embed name and RPC argument used in this range exists. Ran four Playwright experiments with fresh signup accounts against the live app (the shared gateway on :54321 had already crashed before I started — see info finding — so I ran a private gateway on :54391 over the same PostgREST/DB/JWT secret; nothing shared was restarted or deleted) plus SQL in a scratch DB. Verdict: the data wiring (paging, optimistic writes, escaping of adapter-built markup, schema contract) is sound, but there are three serious problems: (1) stored XSS — profile avatar_url, post_media.url and the comment author's headline reach unescaped slots of the approved renderPost/av() markup, any user can store them, and the session (access + refresh token) sits in localStorage under 'dx-auth' → account takeover of anyone who views the feed, including admins; (2) a saved session does not survive a page load in practice — the demo's endSplash timer re-shows #authWrap 700 ms after the adapter opened the app, so the login page is laid over the app on every reload (screenshot taken); (3) logout does not clear per-user caches, so the next person on the same browser inherits the previous user's liked/saved flags and their first like is recorded as an un-like. Plus: no onAuthStateChange (multi-tab logout / expired session leave the app in a broken anonymous state showing raw RLS errors), demo seed data (posts, people, stats) leaks into the live app, and a transient profile fetch failure at load signs the user out.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Schema contract: every table/column/FK-embed/RPC used in lines 1-430 exists in the live DB — profiles (location, followers_count, open_to_work, hiring, profile_views, avatar_url…), posts (share_count, view_count, pinned), post_media, reactions (kind 'like' allowed), comments, saved_posts, connections, notifications, messages (attachment jsonb, read_at); embeds posts_user_id_fkey, comments_user_id_fkey, connections_requester_fkey/_addressee_fkey, notifications_from_user_fkey; RPCs suggest_people(p_limit), my_network_stats(), my_conversations(p_limit), conversation_messages(p_partner,p_before_id,p_limit), new_messages(p_after) with matching argument names; post category select values match posts_category_check.; Feed keyset paging (created_at desc, id desc with the or= cursor): walked all pages in the live DB — 4 pages, 82 posts loaded, 82 unique, equal to select count(*) (82, ids 1-85 with deleted gaps); appended pages are de-duplicated by id.; Id mapping aid()/uuidOf(): deterministic within a session (same uuid → same local id), ids start at 1001 so renderNetwork's >1000 filter and the demo's parseInt/strict-equality lookups work; gotoProfile fetches unseen people first; no overflow risk.; Adapter-built markup escapes server text: message bubbles (esc2 body, initials), thread rows (name, preview via textContent), notifications (actor name, message), attachment names/paths; attachment paths are also constrained by messages_attachment_path; signed URLs come from createSignedUrls, never from stored strings.; Optimistic writes revert on error (like/save/comment/connect/accept); deletePost and clearAllNotifs respect the interface's undo window before touching the DB; posts insert uses user_id = ME_UUID and RLS rejects anything else (verified the reactions RLS rejection path).; Session cleanup on logout for live channels: timer cleared, sb.removeAllChannels(), MX state reset; schedule() stops when ME_UUID is null; tick() skips hidden tabs; logout removes 'dx-auth' from localStorage (verified).; Wrap-after-render rule (CLAUDE.md) respected for messages: mxOpenThread re-wrapped on every goto('messages') guarded by __live, sendMessage reassigned each time; renderMessages/renderNetwork wrappers are resolved by global name so the demo's goto() reaches them.; Auth UX basics: client-side password ≥ 8 (server enforces too), uniform 'Wrong email or password' on login, buttons disabled while a request is in flight, e-mail trimmed; live build hides .lg-demo and [data-sim]; live.py refuses a service_role key in the browser.; Live-stack experiments used only fresh accounts (rv-adapter1-*@qa.test) and my own posts; no shared data was modified or deleted; private gateway stopped and scratch DB rv_adapter1 dropped at the end.

**ما اتغطاش:** The shared gateway on :54321 had already crashed (ERR_OUT_OF_RANGE → ERR_HTTP_HEADERS_SENT, see info finding) before my first request, so browser experiments ran against a private gateway instance on :54391 using the same PostgREST :3001, DB drugbox_live and JWT secret (identical behaviour, different port); I did not restart the shared process.; Real Supabase Auth/Realtime behaviours (e-mail confirmation, refresh-token rotation and revocation, Realtime channel authorisation/RLS on postgres_changes) cannot be exercised on the local emulator; findings on those paths (onAuthStateChange, signOut on hydrate failure) are code-path arguments plus local reproductions of the client-side part.; E1a uploads (uploadPostMedia/sendAttachments/signAll) were reviewed by reading only; no files were uploaded to the shared storage to keep the run light.; Lines 430-1268 (companies, deals, listings, jobs, trust, groups, documents, moderation, checkout, training, profile page wrappers) were only skimmed for interactions with auth/id-mapping; they belong to other agents.; The initial 'flash' of demo posts on a fresh page load before the first feed page arrives was not timed (the failure case and the sidebar/search leaks were reproduced instead).

### adapter-3
Read the full adapter region and every layer it wraps (videos.js, checkout.js, moderation.js, base app.html jx/gx/training/create-group code) and cross-checked every PostgREST select/embed and RPC name/arg against the live PostgREST on :3001, every bucket/trigger/constraint against drugbox_live, and ran two live experiments with fresh signup accounts (a Playwright browser against localhost:54321 and benign REST calls). I did NOT run the e2e scripts and did NOT alter the shared DB (created rows were deleted; my browsers were closed). Verdict: the data-plumbing is mostly correct and server-side authorization for moderation/payments/groups is genuinely enforced by RLS (not client-gated), BUT I found one CRITICAL cross-user stored XSS reachable through the live media path, one HIGH privacy defect (private groups are silently created public), and several medium/low correctness and robustness issues. The headline XSS was reproduced end-to-end: a normal user who sets their own profile intro_video.poster to a crafted string runs arbitrary JS in any other user's browser (including a moderator's) when they view that user's candidate card / profile.

**اتفحص وسليم:** dxPay.start sends only {product, method, company_id, listing_id} — no amount; create_order (0018:44) recomputes amount_cents from payment_products and enforces ownership (vip→company owner/admin, boost/featured→own listing). Amounts/prices are never trusted from the browser.; confirm_payment/activate_order/set_order_provider_ref are security definer and revoked from public/anon/authenticated (granted only to service_role); review_instapay and submit_instapay enforce is_platform_admin / auth.uid() ownership server-side. Verified RPCs reject anon callers (my_interactions, submit_instapay, review_instapay all returned 401/42501 as anon).; Moderation (Admin → Review) actions are enforced by the DB, not just the client: verification_requests UPDATE requires is_platform_admin (0001:1361), moderate_reference requires role in (admin,moderator), site_certificates checked_at update is admin-only, and dxModeration.decide throws 'Only the Drugbox team can do this' when .select() returns 0 rows. A non-admin cannot act even though the Review nav is added for everyone.; Anonymous reviewer identity does NOT leak to the client: get_reviews_many (0012:6) returns author = CASE WHEN anonymous THEN null, and never returns the reviewer uuid (only reviewee); the raw job_reviews table is author-read-only by RLS. The adapter maps null → 'Anonymous'.; Public buckets are constrained server-side: videos allows only video/* + image/jpeg, post-media allows images/pdf/office/zip (no svg/html) — so stored-XSS via uploading an HTML/SVG file to a public bucket is blocked by allowed_mime_types (verified in storage.buckets). The gateway returns 415 on a disallowed content-type.; Redirect/iframe handling is safe: result() navigates only to r.checkout_url which is built server-side by payments-create from PAYMOB_BASE; the return handler parses ?payment=(\d+) only; no postMessage/origin handling is present to abuse.; All PostgREST select/embeds used in this region resolve against the live schema (work_references au/ca fkey embeds, job_lists t embed, verification_requests co, site_certificates st/co, company_reports co + rp fkey, payment_orders pr+u, jobs poster fkey, groups/training/products/enrollments/followers) — every query returned HTTP 200.; No N+1 in the trust/groups/training loaders: loadTrust batches reviews via get_reviews_many (two calls) and a single work_references/job_lists/my_interactions Promise.all; groups/training each use one query pair. No realtime channels are opened in this region (realtime is confined to B4 messages).; Group create/join/leave/delete authorization is DB-enforced: joining a private group and reading its members as an outsider are both rejected by RLS (verified: 403 + empty read), and deleting another user's group affects 0 rows (adapter throws 'Only the group's creator can delete it').; wrap-after-render is correctly implemented: checkout wrapBoost guards openBoostModal with __pay and re-wraps via dxCore.onRender('checkout', wrapBoost); the jobs adapter re-wraps submitApply/openPostJobModal/submitGeneric with __live on every renderJobs (+ setTimeout). The boost pay button is matched by #bmPayBtn id (language-independent), so boost checkout survives Arabic.; jobCard apply-btn onclick is built with JSON.stringify(title/company) and assigned via setAttribute (not innerHTML), so the values become valid JS string literals with no HTML re-parse — no injection there (the inline code comment's reasoning checks out). Card text fields use textContent or esc2().; CV/verification/payments document paths are path-scoped and private: can_read_document/can_write_document (0016, 0018) restrict cv/ to owner+employer-of-applied-job, verification/ to company owners/admins+Drugbox, payments/ to owner+Drugbox; submit_instapay enforces the receipt path prefix payments/<uid>/.

**ما اتغطاش:** Did not run the e2e Playwright suites (instructed not to); live experiments used only fresh signup accounts in my own browser contexts.; The Arabic group-create breakage (finding 3) was reasoned from the code + i18n dictionary, not executed in an Arabic-locale browser — marked PLAUSIBLE.; Did not test real Paymob/Fawry/InstaPay provider calls (payments-create's provider branches) — provider secrets are absent and that server code is another agent's area; I only reviewed the browser-facing dxPay contract.; The broader root of finding 4 (no live editor for profiles.experience/open_to_work) sits in the profile/B area outside my assigned lines; I verified only the trust-layer consequence.; Did not exhaustively fuzz every jsonb column for other unescaped render sinks beyond intro_video.poster; other profile free-text fields are rendered via esc2/textContent in the paths I read, but a full sink inventory across all shared layers was out of scope.; Did not assess the company-reports design question (0017 RLS lets a company owner/admin resolve/dismiss reports filed against their own company) — it is a deliberate DB policy, possibly intended; flagged here as an observation rather than a confirmed defect.

### fe-core
Read all 12 files line by line plus the base app wiring they hook (goto, DBK kit, applyFilters, openGroup, deletePost, keydown) from base64-stripped copies, and the live adapter boot/USERS/company code. Ran Playwright against file:///home/user/new/web/dist/drugbox.html (parity_check: PARITY OK): a click-through crawler on all 12 pages at 1440px (660 controls) and 375px (124 controls) plus every dx feature (Ctrl+K, theme modes, tour, undo delete/clear, chat dock, compare, quick view, chips, saved searches, For-you roles/cards, honeycomb, insights, hover card, 👤/📞, groups join/open, jobs filters, Arabic switch) — zero pageerrors and zero console errors except the Google Fonts fetch blocked by the sandbox proxy. Targeted hypothesis scripts (hyp.py, focus.py, probe2–5.py, ux.py) with screenshots in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-new/821674db-a196-55af-97b7-080b6c8aa6d4/scratchpad/fe-core/. Verdict: the layers are stable at runtime and mostly escape correctly, but links.js has raw-HTML sinks that become stored cross-user XSS in the live build (company phone/WhatsApp → 📞 dialog; USERS name/headline → ‘N reposts’ dialog); several demo-only simulations (fake chat auto-reply with undelivered messages, hash-assigned ‘Price dropped / Expiring in 3 days’ badges, fabricated profile insights naming demo people) ship to production with no live hook or flag; a11y.css recolours the navy ticker to 1.5–3.2:1 contrast; many dx-layer strings stay English in Arabic; the search ‘Ctrl K’ badge covers typed text in both directions; tapping the verified seal on job cards jumps to Groups; and the Appearance popover / Ctrl+K / tour lack keyboard management. Several hypotheses were disproved by testing and are recorded under checked_ok.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Runtime errors: 0 pageerrors and 0 app console errors across all 12 pages (feed, network, messages, notifs, profile, saved, groups, market, jobs, companies, training, admin) with click-through at 1440px (660 controls) and 375px (124 controls), plus the feature exercise (Ctrl+K open/type/Enter, dark/light/fs/season themes on 3 pages, tour, undo delete + undo clear notifications, chat dock send/min/full, compare 3 listings, quick view, glossary hover/focus, chips + save/apply/delete search, For-you roles and cards, honeycomb endorse, insights viewer, hover card Message, 👤/📞, groups join/leave/open, jobs filters untick-all/Clear, Arabic switch and back). Only failed request: fonts.googleapis.com (blocked by the sandbox proxy, graceful fallback).; XSS surface of the 12 files: no eval/new Function/document.write/javascript: hrefs; the only target=_blank (links.js L53) has rel=noopener; esc() applied in hover card, quick view, dock names/messages, Ctrl+K results and empty text, chips, saved-search names, cert wall, honeycomb, For-you items, insights; Ctrl+K typed payload and #searchIn payload rendered inert; quick view copies existing DOM innerHTML (price/seller/stats) which is already escaped markup; brand/craft inserted SVG/illustrations are static.; Filter chips (batch2.js L216-251): removal does re-apply visibility because the base exposes window.updateLiveCount = applyFilters (app.html L4198); after × the visible set equals a real untick (8/10, the base’s ‘defaults apply once touched’ rule), ‘Clear all’ restores the full 15/15; no stale state.; Jobs employment-type/country filters (links.js L99-135): all 4 hunting cards are Full-time so unticking Full-time correctly empties the list with the ‘No jobs match’ state; Remote-only shows exactly the one Remote card; Clear filters restores.; Tour (craft.js): does not restart when #app attributes change while it is open (step stays at 1); first-run guard and dx_tour_done respected; targets skipped when off-screen on mobile.; a11y names (a11y.js): zero buttons left with the generic ‘Button’ label and zero inputs with container-derived labels ≥50 chars on feed, market, jobs, messages, profile, groups, companies.; Mobile 375px: no horizontal scroll on any page (document/body/content scrollWidth = 375); dock (bottom 748), compare tray (682-734), Ctrl+K sheet and theme popover (65-325) stay inside the viewport and above the bottom nav (top 756); screenshots m_*.png.; Dark mode (brand.css A5): computed contrast of dx-layer text after invert+hue-rotate+screen transform passes AA except the two rules already reported (ticker, .in-person em); photos/logo flipped back correctly (dark_feed.png, dark_market.png); #lightbox and #toast live outside #app so the filter does not re-parent them.; Listener/timer hygiene: wrappers are guarded (applyJobFilters.__dx, data-dx*/dataset flags, onclick= assignment instead of addEventListener on re-rendered bars), timers cleared (undo, hover, loader), MutationObservers created once; render bus slices ≤8 ms with the heaviest single run 14.7 ms (count-up) and icons 2.6 ms average over 173 runs on mobile.; Escape handling of DBK modals and Ctrl+K works; core closes cached marketplace/jobs/groups windows on navigation; base Join/Message/Endorse handlers still fire through the capture-phase layers (stopPropagation at document does not block same-node listeners).; Parity: python3 tools/parity_check.py web/reference/demo-approved.html web/dist/drugbox.html → PARITY OK; the live build embeds all 10 layer script tags plus the adapter.; RTL layout of layer widgets: For-you panel, insights, cert wall, dock, state badges, head illustration (no overlap with the page title at 1440px) render acceptably with dir=rtl; theme-button/seal margins use physical sides (cosmetic only).

**ما اتغطاش:** Live stack reproduction (http://localhost:54321) was not run: the gateway has no seeded login and signing up would insert rows into the shared drugbox_live database, so live-impact statements rest on the identical build (web/dist/live/index.html) and adapter.js code paths rather than an executed session.; No axe-core WCAG sweep (no network to fetch it and /tmp/axe.min.js is absent); contrast was computed by my own script for layer elements only — the approved base page’s own colours were not audited.; Speed before/after (tests/demo/legacy/stress_base.py vs stress.py) was not measured; only per-feature render timings from dxCore.perf were read.; Screen-reader behaviour (aria-live announcements of undo/toast/tooltip) was not tested with assistive technology; findings are based on DOM attributes and focus order.; Part-2 layers (directory*, hub-*, molecules, share, tiers, videos, i18n) were read only where these files call into them (dxOrigText, dxDir.companies, applyFilters wiring); their own sinks are out of scope here.; The afford.js 380 ms fallback was probed on feed, market, jobs, groups and network text elements only; other pages’ plain-text clicks were not exhaustively tried.

### fe-directory
Read all 13 files in scope plus the pieces they depend on (hub-ui.js render path, DBK.modal/requireFields in web/base/app.html, i18n.js, live/adapter.js company mapping and store hook, migrations 0001/0006 for companies RLS + companies_guard). Important structural fact: in the demo `dxHub.render()` always returns true, so directory.js's own list/company rendering (#drQ, [data-go], renderDirectory/renderCompany), directory2.js pageExtras/whoMakes/pickBoxes and directory3.js tools() are dead code; what stays live from these files are the dialogs, editor, store()/companies(), logo()/pimg(), share, videos, landed cost and profposts. Ran 8 Playwright batteries against file:///home/user/new/web/dist/drugbox.html (XSS sinks with hostile strings across every dialog/tab, functional logic, layout at 375/768/1440 in EN and AR with screenshots, Arabic-mode string coverage, search normalisation, videos, performance with 1/3 uploaded covers and 517 companies), one scratch-database RLS proof (rv_fedir, dropped), and one live end-to-end exploit on the shared stack (hostile logo_url stored by an owner via the public API executed in another user's browser; row/users deleted afterwards). Verdict: HTML escaping of text content is thorough (35/35 hostile-string checks passed, modal titles escaped), search is solid, but there is one critical stored-XSS sink (company logo/cover URLs rendered raw in directory.js logo() and the cover style), a severe performance cliff (every render re-reads and JSON-parses localStorage hundreds of times, so one uploaded cover photo makes the directory 18x slower and typing takes 0.3–1.9 s per key), a lost verification request for claimed companies in the demo, wrong-company attribution for share/video/trade-show when names share a prefix, and the editor/share/landed/brochure UIs are English in Arabic mode. Scripts and screenshots: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-new/821674db-a196-55af-97b7-080b6c8aa6d4/scratchpad/fe-directory/ (t1_xss.py … t8_misc.py, shots/).

**اتفحص وسليم:** Text/attribute escaping: 35/35 hostile-string checks ("><img onerror>, <svg onload>, quotes, &amp;, <b>) across company creation wizard, review step, editor fields and product rows, every company tab, directory cards, product dialog (title = product name), RFQ dialog (title/options), deals centre, share card caption, trade-show mode, QR dialog, topbar company switch — no injection, no JS errors (t1_xss.py).; DBK.modal escapes title/aria-label/buttons (app.html: esc(o.title)); requireFields validates emails; toasts escape text.; Directory search (hub path used by the demo): no RegExp built from user input anywhere in scope (only escaped keyword regexes in brand.js/batch2.js); special characters ( [ * \ .* (?: and a 10k-char query cause no errors; Arabic alef variants (أقراص/اقراص), tashkeel (ميتفورمِين), taa-marbuta, case (METFORMIN) and surrounding whitespace normalised; Arabic company name كوادرا finds Quadra Pharm; focus and caret position survive the debounced full re-render; 'Who makes' panel appears for a 4+ letter ingredient; zero results show the empty state with a working Clear filters.; Contact links: website entered as javascript:alert(1) becomes https://javascript:… (inert); tel:/mailto:/wa.me/Google-Maps links use esc()/encodeURIComponent; realistic 52-char email and 49-char website wrap correctly at 375 px.; Videos: 101 MB file refused instantly by size before any decoding; garbage MP4 refused within seconds with the H.264 hint; MKV refused; WebM upload captures poster frame + duration; nothing is loaded until Play (no <video> element before); Enter on the Play button opens the player; Escape closes it; replace/remove work (company limit 3 min correctly accepts a 1:35 video); IndexedDB persistence as per existing videos_test.; Share/trade-show: 1200×630 PNG, WhatsApp link carries encodeURIComponent(caption), download filename sanitised to [a-z0-9-]; trade-show entered by click → fullscreen, Escape exits, interval cleared, no overlay or <video> left behind; QR SVG from qrcode.min.js with escaped slug in the download name.; Group buying: target 0 does not produce NaN/Infinity in the progress bar; joining requires acting as a company; surplus/dossier flows create deals through dxDeals.create (own-company requests refused).; Performance baseline (17 companies): directory render 25 ms, keystroke re-render 4–10 ms, companies→feed switch 21 ms, feed→market 32 ms — page switching is not slowed by the directory itself; the regressions reported above appear only once covers/logos are stored or the list grows.; Layout: at 375/768/1440 in EN and AR the directory list, products tab, editor drawer (fits viewport), finder, share and RFQ dialogs (with normal-length names) and trade-show mode show no horizontal overflow; the right-to-left search input and hb-ar Arabic names render correctly.; profposts.js: composer greeting is safe against $' / $& replacement patterns in the user's name (only one 'What's on your mind' and no leaked markup); Activity tab wrapper falls back to the original tab on error.; Live database guards (observed while reproducing): companies_guard blocks status/plan/owner/licensed changes by owners; directory_companies is security invoker; the adapter compensates the verification dialog for real companies (adapter.js:1115-1127).; No page errors in any battery; console only shows blocked external resource loads from the network-abort route.

**ما اتغطاش:** hub-ui.js / hub-data.js internals (ranking, workspace, sites, team, reviews) beyond what the directory page and my modules exercise — assumed to be another agent's area; the cover sink and per-card mine() cost are reported here only because the sink/entry point lives in directory.js.; tiers.js shares the name-prefix bug (line 53) — noted in the share/videos finding, not separately tested.; Native Web Share (navigator.share with files) and real clipboard permission prompts — not available in headless Chromium; only the fallback paths were exercised.; Actual print output of the Profile PDF (brochure) — only the popup's HTML/text and language were checked.; Real mobile devices/touch, Safari/iOS behaviour (HEVC is a documented known limit), screen-reader output beyond ARIA attribute presence.; Pagination/infinite-scroll boundaries — there is no paging to test (all companies render at once; reported as a finding).; The live verification dialog end to end (t9_live_verify.py written but not run) — adapter.js:1115-1127 explicitly handles it, and the shared stack was not loaded further.; Supabase Storage upload of logos/covers in the live adapter (whether the editor's data-URL logos are stored as URLs or paths) — only the DB/RPC/DOM chain for logo_url and profile.cover was proven.

### fe-hub-deals
Read all 13 files plus the code they depend on (DBK kit modal in web/base/app.html, directory.js upgrade/plan dialogs, openBoostModal, i18n.js, live/adapter.js, migrations 0006/0007/0008/0017/0018). Tested with Playwright on the demo (web/dist/drugbox.html; 4 scripts, logged in and not, desktop 1366 and mobile 375, EN and AR) and on the shared live stack (signup of 3 throw-away users, cleaned up afterwards), and with SQL on a private scratch DB rv_fehub (dropped at the end). Verdict: the deal state machine, VIP arithmetic, text escaping in deal views and the mobile layout are sound, but there are two critical problems that are reachable by any normal user in the live app — self-promotion to platform admin through the profiles update policy (confirmed end-to-end: Admin → Review queues opened and review_instapay passed the admin gate) and a stored XSS through company cover/logo rendered unescaped by hub-ui.js — plus a trust-layer spoof in tiers.js (an unverified company named "Quadra Pharm Egypt Ltd" shows Level 3 · Inspected and Quadra Pharm's passport), an O(n²) directory render (3.5 s at 1,017 companies, ~11 s search latency; live loads up to 5,000) and a boost price that is displayed from a live FX rate (١٬٦٣٦) while the server charges 1,653 from payment_products.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Deal state machine: dxDeals.FLOWS (steps/acts for all 7 types) compared programmatically with public.deal_flow() from the database — identical; NEXT matches deal_next_status() including the service 'accept' special case. Wrong-side actions ('from' sending a quote) and out-of-turn steps refused; full quote flow sent→countered→quoted→accepted→confirmed→shipped→delivered→closed reproduced in the demo.; Expired offers: accept refused in the UI when validity has elapsed ('0 days'), mirroring deal_act's offer_at check; rating clamped to 1–5 before the review is stored (DB validates stars too).; VIP arithmetic: monthly 2,500 + VAT 350 = 2,850 and yearly 25,000 + 3,500 = 28,500 in the pay window match payment_products (vip_month/vip_year); create_order charges from payment_products only (165,300 / 450,300 cents verified).; Arabic UI and checkout: 'Pay for VIP' title and 'Pay EGP …' button are not translated, so the checkout.js observer (data-pay) and the live click handler still work in Arabic; the four payment methods are injected in both languages.; XSS in deal views: titles, messages, notes, counter prices, timeline text, company/person names and data-deal attributes are escaped (payloads in title/message/quote attributes did not execute).; Live build hides the demo-only 'Simulate reply' button via CSS (`[data-sim]{display:none!important}` in web/build/live.py:16); a forced click is refused by the engine ('You are not part of this request') and the local state is reloaded.; Admin page: non-admins (ME.role from profiles) get 'Admin access required' and no #adminContent, so the Review wrapper cannot render; server-side decisions are gated by is_platform_admin()/RLS (given a legitimately protected role — see the critical finding).; Mobile 375px: directory, company page (all tabs), workspace (overview/team/plan), deal thread and deals centre, Admin → Review, boost window, upgrade and pay dialogs — no horizontal overflow (document and #content scrollWidth = 375); the Sponsored row is an intentional horizontal scroller (.dr-vs-row overflow-x:auto).; Corrupt JSON in dx_deals / dx_company_edits / dx_reports / dx_passport / dx_meta_* is swallowed by store()'s try/catch and treated as empty — directory (17 cards) and company page render with zero page errors.; Kit dialogs expose role=dialog, aria-modal=true, aria-label=title and an aria-labelled close button; the first field is focused on open; required fields and the email format are enforced (invite with 'abc' rejected).; Destructive workspace actions (Remove site, Remove team member) offer dxUndo after acting.; Deal/report/review text written by users is escaped in hub-ui.js (reports, reviews, replies, activity log, team names, Arabic names).; hex.json and ill.json are static SVG data (hex backgrounds, illustrations) inlined by build.py; no user data flows through them.

**ما اتغطاش:** The Paymob/Fawry stand-in and payment functions (ports 54400/54500, /tmp/drugbox-fn.env) were not running, so the Fawry reference modal, the Paymob redirect/return (?payment=) and InstaPay submission were reviewed from code only, not driven in the browser.; Directory performance was measured in the demo with 1,017 companies; the live app at 5,000 companies was not measured because inserting thousands of rows into the shared drugbox_live database could disturb other agents (the per-card companies() rebuild applies to the live list by the same code path).; Group-buying join/confirm in the live app (groups are handled by the C3 adapter section, outside this area) and the directory2/directory3 dialogs that the hub buttons delegate to (RFQ, questionnaire, surplus, dossier, brochure, QR).; Real Paymob/Fawry provider behaviour, webhook signatures and the server-side payment functions themselves (another agent's area).

### fe-molecules-i18n
Read all five files in scope end to end and tested every claim in a browser or Node. Vendored supabase-js: downloaded @supabase/supabase-js 2.45.4 from the npm registry and compared — web/src/vendor/supabase-2.45.4.min.js is byte-identical to package/dist/umd/supabase.js (sha256 8596965f…01e7); npm's bulk advisory endpoint reports one low advisory in the bundled auth-js 2.65.0 (GHSA-8r88-6cj9-9fh5, admin API only). molecules.js: scripted validation of the 10 MOLS entries (keys, formula, MW, class, SVG well-formedness, no scripts, no duplicates) passed, but the "Structure" chip is appended inside listing-title elements and leaks into share captions ("…GMP GradeStructure"). i18n: exercised dxT() in Node, parsed the dictionary (745 entries, 3 duplicate keys with different values — the sidebar "Saved" item renders as "تم الحفظ"), then walked 29 pages + 30 dialogs in Arabic with Playwright (file:///home/user/new/web/dist/drugbox.html, network blocked) collecting untranslated text/attributes/options, computed text-align/direction, clipping, overflow, fonts, persistence and switch timing, and reviewed 20 screenshots for RTL defects. Results: no horizontal overflow or clipped buttons anywhere; page switching is not slowed by Arabic (208 vs 213 ms summed medians); persistence works; but the marketplace pricing banner is never translated because the SKIP list's ".pb-text" matches the banner (class collision), the login/signup pages have no language toggle and signup is half-translated, the Ctrl+K badge covers the RTL search placeholder, the ticker marquee leaves the right half of the bar empty in RTL, user-generated English text is not bidi-isolated (".Hello Dr. Haytham…", "?contract", "AM 10:32"), the approved boost modal is Arabic-only with Arabic-Indic digits even in the English UI, the Google Fonts stylesheet blocks first paint for as long as the host hangs (6.2 s vs 0.2 s offline), and training/admin/company/deals/molecule screens are half-translated. Overall verdict: the i18n layer is sound in architecture (original text preserved, restore is exact, no logic depends on shown text, no perf cost) but has one real wiring bug (.pb-text), a dictionary-key bug, several RTL layout defects in the approved base, and a long tail of untranslated UI; nothing security-relevant in this area.

**اتفحص وسليم:** supabase-2.45.4.min.js is byte-identical to the npm @supabase/supabase-js@2.45.4 UMD build (sha256 8596965f…01e7); live.py embeds it content-hashed and refuses service_role keys; molecules.js data: 10 entries, lowercase keys, name/formula/mw/cls/svg present, MW values numeric and chemically correct to 2 dp (e.g. metformin 129.17, ciprofloxacin 331.35), SVGs open/close properly, contain no <script>/on*/javascript:, no duplicate names; modal content is static build-time data (no injection surface); click handler is capture-phase with stopPropagation and chips are never injected inside clickable ancestors; qrcode.min.js is the standard qrcode-generator build (window.qrcode(typeNumber, 'M') → createSvgTag) used by share.js:63 and directory2.js:164 with app URLs only; no innerHTML use; i18n.js architecture: original text kept in WeakMaps, toggling back restores English exactly (innerText diff after round-trip only in live counters), html lang and dir updated, window.LANG kept in sync with the base toggleLang, SKIP excludes user content, dxOrigText provided and no code compares displayed (translated) text; no JS errors originate from the i18n layer; Language persistence: dx_lang in localStorage survives reload (login page comes up Arabic), fresh sessions default to English, toggle updates the button label and toasts; No horizontal overflow (#content and document scrollWidth == clientWidth) on 29 pages and 30 dialogs at 1440×900 in RTL; no clipped/nowrap-overflowing buttons or tabs (text expansion did not break any control); Page switching is not slowed by the Arabic walker: summed medians over 11 pages EN 208 ms vs AR 213 ms vs EN-again 207 ms (7 runs each); Translated arrows are mirrored (→↔←) incl. 'Skip →', 'Sign In →', 'View Listing →'; email/password inputs forced LTR with right alignment; RTL touch scrollbar rule present; SVG icons kept LTR; Digits are consistently Latin in the i18n layer (0 Arabic-Indic digits in i18n.js); country/month names translated; Pharma terminology largely correct: سواغات, مواد فعالة, تصنيع لدى الغير, مستحضر نهائي, هيئة الدواء المصرية, السجل التجاري/البطاقة الضريبية, شهادة التحليل, جواز الامتثال levels; build.py syntax-checks each layer with node --check and wraps each in its own <script data-dx> tag so one failure cannot stop the others

**ما اتغطاش:** Security advisory lookups: OSV API and the GitHub Advisory API were blocked (403) by the proxy; only npm's bulk advisory endpoint answered, so advisory coverage of supabase-js and its sub-packages is limited to what npm reports; Real Arabic font rendering on Windows/macOS/Android could not be observed (container Chromium has only DejaVu Sans with Arabic glyphs); font conclusions come from the CSS stacks and document.fonts; Mobile/phone-width RTL layouts were not walked (desktop 1440×900 only); tests/demo/legacy/rtl_scroll.py covers mobile sideways scroll separately; The live build (adapter.js on the local Supabase stack) was not exercised in Arabic; all browser tests used the standalone demo file; Not every one of the 745 dictionary entries was reviewed for naturalness — about 120 entries plus all on-screen text from the walk were checked; the long tail of untranslated strings is listed in scratchpad walk_ar.json rather than exhaustively in the findings

### markup-integrity
Read the base page (stripped of base64), the built demo (web/dist/drugbox.html, 9,555,480 B), the live build and all build/parity scripts. Ran: (1) a custom html.parser scanner over the base and built files (ids, label/aria references, images, buttons, inputs, headings, landmarks, inline handlers); (2) a Playwright runtime audit over 15 rendered screens (splash, login, 12 pages via goto(), company hub, another person's profile, Arabic feed) with all non-file requests blocked, checking duplicate ids, every inline on*-handler callee, accessible names, labels, headings, landmarks, lang/dir, console/page errors and network leaks; (3) load-cost measurements on a mobile viewport: file:// at 1x and 4x CPU throttling, and over HTTP with Slow-4G emulation (1.6 Mbps/150 ms) + 4x CPU; (4) no-JS "lite" mode with JavaScript disabled; (5) data-URI inventory/dedup of the built file and gzip size; (6) grep/semantic review of the inline sample data (people, companies, jobs, listings, prices, dates, phones, e-mails); (7) edge-case executions of parity_check.py and a synthetic service_role JWT against live.py's guard; (8) grep of dist/live for keys/source maps. Overall: the rendered markup is in good shape (no duplicate ids, no missing handler functions, no unlabeled controls/fields, no console errors), but the page is not actually offline/leak-free (render-blocking Google Fonts link for fonts that are never used), the live boost checkout displays a USD×FX price that differs from the server price, the 9.5 MB single document (64% one base64 video) costs ~47 s to interactive on Slow 4G, live.py's service_role guard cannot catch a real service_role JWT, and parity_check accepts degenerate/empty inputs as PARITY OK. Sample data contains stale 2025 deadlines rendered as open and a few real-world identities.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Runtime DOM on 15 screens (splash, login, feed, market, companies, jobs, network, messages, notifs, profile, groups, training, saved, admin, company hub, another person's profile, Arabic feed): 0 duplicate ids (87–139 ids per screen); Every inline on*-handler callee in the rendered DOM resolves to a function on each page (110 distinct names in the whole file; page-scoped ones are defined on render, as CLAUDE.md describes); 0 visible <img> without an alt attribute; 0 buttons/links/tabs without an accessible name (up to 84 controls per page); 0 form fields without a label/aria-label (up to 25 fields per page); no positive tabindex; skip link present; no heading-level skips except the company hub; <meta charset> within the first 1 KB, viewport `width=device-width,initial-scale=1,viewport-fit=cover` (zoom allowed), <title> present, html lang=en; switching to Arabic sets html lang=ar dir=rtl; Arabic lite texts carry lang=ar dir=rtl; No console errors or uncaught exceptions during load, login and the page tour (only the blocked Google Fonts resource error and headless WebGL warnings); Only one external request at load (Google Fonts, reported above); no analytics/tag managers/CDN scripts; exchangerate.host is only touched when the boost modal opens; no fetch/XHR/sendBeacon elsewhere in the demo; dist/live embeds exactly one JWT whose payload role is 'anon'; no 'service_role' string, no second JWT, no sourceMappingURL in index.html or js/*.js; adapter and supabase-js are content-hashed copies identical to web/src; build.py syntax-checks each module with node --check and emits one <script data-dx> per module; CSS layers are inserted before the first </style>; parity_check on the current build: dist and reference identical outside the excluded lite snapshot (first diff = weekday word); live/index.html = demo + 533 injected bytes; Sample-data cross-references: every uid in POSTS, COMMENTS, PRODUCTS, ENQUIRIES, JOBS, COMPANIES, NOTIFS, MSGS and CONN_STATE resolves to USERS 1–7; no negative prices; salary currencies match job locations; group-buying deadlines (2026-10-30, 2026-11-10) are in the future; no 14-digit national-id-like numbers; most phones/e-mails are synthetic (.example, 2010000000xx); No eval/new Function/javascript: URLs/string timers/iframes — a CSP without 'unsafe-eval' is possible; No-JS lite mode works: app hidden, 13 lite sections shown, no 'needs a browser' alert, splash fades by CSS; Load cost on fast hardware: file:// domInteractive 543 ms, FCP 192 ms, JS heap ~10 MB, login→feed 0.6 s

**ما اتغطاش:** Outbound network is blocked in this audit, so the current behaviour of api.exchangerate.host (whether it still answers without an access key) was not observed; the demo falls back to 49.50 either way; Slow-4G timings were taken over a local python http.server without compression on a 4-CPU server with 4x CPU throttling; real phones/CDN compression will differ (gzip figure computed offline: 5.83 MB); Desktop three.js globe path was not profiled under throttling (it is skipped on phones by design); No offline HTML validator (bs4/lxml/html5lib absent); markup checks were DOM-based on rendered pages plus a custom html.parser scan, not a full W3C validation of every generated template; tests/demo and tests/e2e suites were not re-run (other agents' scope); the Supabase adapter, RLS and payment functions were only read where needed to trace the price display; The lite snapshot's clickable-overlay heuristics (snapshot.py) were not exhaustively tested for wrong destinations

### infra-gateway
ملخص سريع: الـ gateway المحلي فيه مشكلتين كبار (طلب GET واحد بهيدر Range غلط بيقفّل السيرفر كله، وثغرة path traversal في محاكاة الـ Storage بتكتب/تمسح ملفات برّه مجلد التخزين كـ root)، وبيشتغل على 0.0.0.0، وسكريبت start.sh بيسجّل PID غلط وبيعلّق أي حاجة بتلتقط الـ output. مفيش أسرار حقيقية في الريبو، وnpm audit نضيف. الدوكس فيها تضارب (DATA-CONTRACT قديم، متغيرات ملف env للـ payments مش موثّقة).

IMPORTANT OPERATIONAL NOTE: while probing read-only, a single GET with `Range: bytes=999999999-` on a public video CRASHED the shared gateway on :54321 (log: ERR_OUT_OF_RANGE then ERR_HTTP_HEADERS_SENT in the catch-all, Node exited). PostgreSQL (:5433) and PostgREST (:3001) are untouched and answering; only `node gateway.mjs` is gone. My attempt to relaunch it with the identical configuration was denied by the permission system, so :54321 is DOWN until someone runs (same config as start.sh, does not drop the DB): `cd /home/user/new/tools/local-supabase && PORT=54321 REST_URL=http://127.0.0.1:3001 JWT_SECRET="$(sed -n 's/^jwt-secret = "\(.*\)"/\1/p' /tmp/drugbox-pgrst.conf)" DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:5433/drugbox_live APP_DIR=/home/user/new/web/dist/live setsid nohup node gateway.mjs >> /tmp/drugbox-gateway.log 2>&1 < /dev/null & echo $! > /tmp/drugbox-gateway.pid`. Existing access tokens stay valid (same secret); the in-memory refresh tokens are lost, so browsers already signed in will be signed out at their next refresh; e2e suites sign in fresh.

Method: read all nine files in the area line by line; probed the running gateway with curl (CORS, /js traversal, auth validation paths that do not insert, expired/garbage/forged tokens compared against PostgREST directly, public/signed storage routes, Range); built a private scratch database (stub + all 19 migrations) and ran my own gateway instance on :54399 to reproduce the storage path traversal (write, overwrite, read-back, delete outside STORE_DIR) and the stub's storage.foldername()/claims fidelity gaps; reproduced the start.sh `$!`/pipe-hang behaviour with a harmless scratch script and confirmed it live (pid file 723 = bash wrapper, node = 736); ran the README verification recipe and schema_sweep on a second scratch DB; npm audit via the lock file; git grep secret scan of the whole repo and history; docs-vs-schema comparison. Scratch DBs dropped, my scratch gateway killed, no background processes of mine remain.

Verdict: the gateway is faithful enough for RLS (rows are inserted as the user, policies really decide — proven by an RLS violation once the real foldername() was installed), but it has two serious robustness/security defects (crash on bad Range, path traversal) amplified by binding to all interfaces, hides three production behaviours the e2e suite should see (expired JWT → 401 instead of anon; PostgREST max-rows=1000 and statement timeouts; the real foldername() semantics), and start.sh's process management is broken (wrong pid, stdout hang, kills every PostgREST on the host). Secrets: none in git; only local-stack secrets sit world-readable in /tmp. Docs: DATA-CONTRACT.md and parts of README are stale; the payments env file is undocumented.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Secret scan of the whole repo and git history (git grep for eyJ…, sk_/egy_/pk_/AKIA/ghp_/AIza, service_role, password/secret/token assignments, URLs with credentials, *.supabase.co, base64 blobs; git log -S'eyJ'; added-file history for .env/secret): no real secrets. The only hits are placeholders in PAYMENTS.md, the test value egy_pk_test_local in e4_payments_test.py, the loopback-only authenticator password `local-only`, and `eyJ…` strings inside the HTML bundles that decode to binary video/image data. No .env files in the tree or history; single commit.; npm audit (tools/local-supabase, lock file, registry reachable): 0 vulnerabilities across 13 packages; pg 8.13.1 pinned (latest 8.23.1, no advisory).; web/build/live.py refuses to embed a key containing 'service_role' and only embeds the anon key + URL; built assets are content-hashed (adapter.<sha>.js, supabase.<sha>.js), so the gateway's 1-year immutable cache header on /js/ is safe.; Static file serving: /js/ route regex `^/js/[\w.-]+\.js$` excludes slashes and percent-escapes; `/js/..%2Findex.html` and `/js/%2e%2e%2findex.html` → 404; `/js/../index.html` is normalised by the URL parser to `/index.html` (served, no escape from APP_DIR); unknown paths → 404.; Storage read paths honour bucket visibility: private bucket via /object/public/… → 404; signed GET with a bad signature → 400 'Invalid signature'; signed-URL tokens are HMAC-bound to bucket+name+exp; public GET returns the stored mimetype, accept-ranges and valid 206 for in-range requests; storage POST/DELETE require role=authenticated; bucket file_size_limit and allowed_mime_types are enforced (exact match; buckets use explicit lists, no wildcards, so equal to Supabase).; RLS is genuinely applied in the storage emulation: object rows are inserted/deleted inside a transaction with set_config(sub) + `set local role authenticated`, and installing Supabase's real foldername() in the scratch DB produced a real 'new row violates row-level security policy' error through the same path. No storage policy or public function reads auth.role()/auth.jwt(), so the missing claims are latent only.; JWT handling: HS256 with the same secret as PostgREST, exp checked, HMAC compared with timingSafeEqual, no alg confusion (alg header ignored, HMAC always), signed-storage tokens (2 parts) cannot be replayed as JWTs (3 parts, different HMAC input). Sign-up lower-cases and validates email, enforces 8+ chars (adapter enforces the same), bcrypt via pgcrypto; wrong password → 400 invalid_grant like GoTrue.; CORS: `access-control-allow-origin: *` etc. on OPTIONS and responses — same policy as Supabase; the app is served from the same origin so no credentialed cross-origin use.; PostgreSQL 16 bound to 127.0.0.1 (-c listen_addresses=127.0.0.1) and PostgREST to 127.0.0.1:3001 with db-anon-role=anon and the schema limited to public.; README verification recipe works as documented: stub + 0001 + company_hub.rls.sql prints TOTAL 23 / 23 (also with all 19 migrations); schema_sweep.sql on a fresh build prints the three 'none' lines; all migrations 0001–0019 apply with ON_ERROR_STOP on a fresh database; all migration/test/e2e file names in README and CLAUDE.md exist.; functions-local.js imports resolve (supabase/functions/*/index.js exist); payments-create forwards the user JWT to PostgREST (`create_order` as the user) so RLS/auth.uid() apply locally as on Supabase; the gateway's /functions/v1 proxy only forwards content-type + authorization.; Adapter upload paths all have ≥3 segments (posts/<uid>/<file>, <sender>/<receiver>/<file>, cv|payments/<uid>/<file>, verification/<id>/<file>, people/<uid>/<file>, companies/<id>/<file>) so the stub/real foldername() difference does not change any current policy outcome; sign-up handles a null session ('Check your email…') so an email-confirmation project would not break the flow.; Scratch resources cleaned up: rv_infra_gateway and rv_infra_readme dropped, my gateway on :54399 killed, scratch traversal files removed; no background processes of mine remain (rv_dbpay / rv_scale belong to other agents and were left untouched).

**ما اتغطاش:** The shared gateway on :54321 is DOWN as a side effect of this audit (the Range-header crash, finding 1); relaunching it was denied by the permission system, so it must be relaunched by the orchestrator with the command in the summary. PostgreSQL and PostgREST are intact. Any e2e agent running after ~01:57 UTC will fail until then.; Behaviour of a second `start.sh` run (stale gateway + new secret) was argued from the code and the live pid-file mismatch, not executed, because it drops the shared database and kills the shared PostgREST.; Realtime is not emulated at all (documented in README; DRUGBOX_REALTIME=0 locally) — nothing to probe.; The payment functions and the Paymob/Fawry mock were not running during the audit (ports 54400/54500 closed, /tmp/drugbox-fn.env absent), so the /functions/v1 proxy and start-payments.sh were reviewed by reading only; functions/webhook logic itself is another agent's area.; Rate limiting, email delivery, OTP/magic links and real Supabase Kong behaviour (apikey enforcement, max-rows) are stated from Supabase's documented defaults, not measured against a real project (no project exists yet — E5).; No users or objects were created in the shared drugbox_live database; the traversal and RLS experiments ran on a private scratch database and a private gateway instance.; Only pg is a dependency of the gateway; Deno-side dependencies of the functions (none, no deno.lock) and the vendored supabase-js 2.45.4 bundle were not audited (outside this area).

### tests-quality
READ: all 20 e2e suites, 2 demo suites, 51 legacy demo scripts, the 3 RLS SQL suites, schema_sweep, the stub, migrations 0001/0006/0007/0008/0018, the local gateway and start scripts. RAN (scratch DB rv_tq built from the stub + 0001–0019, dropped afterwards): the three RLS suites + sweep as baseline (23/23, 18/18, 17/17, three 'none'), then 14 deliberate sabotages on template copies, each followed by the matching suite; two legacy demo suites against the current build; Python/SQL reproductions for the vacuous-assertion claims. Did NOT run the e2e suites (another agent).

MUTATION CHECK (sabotage → suite result): M0 RLS off on companies → FAIL detected · M1 companies UPDATE using(true) → detected · M2 grant select(file_path) on company_documents → detected · M3 drop trg_company_members_guard → detected · M4 verification self-approve (trigger admin check removed + open update policy) → NOT detected, but neutralised by companies_guard (0006) so no hole opened (defence in depth) · M5 certificate self-check (trigger + policy with-check removed) → detected · M6 deals SELECT using(true) → detected · M7 deal_act membership branch removed → suite ABORTED with no report; with per-statement rollback detected by 'wrong side'/'out of turn' assertions, while the assertion named for it ('an outsider cannot act on it') stays PASS in both RLS-intact and RLS-open variants (vacuous) · M8 offer-expiry check removed → aborted / detected with rollback · M9 direct UPDATE on deals granted → aborted / detected with rollback · M10 listing INSERT with check(true) → detected · M11 approved_suppliers SELECT using(true) → detected · M12 deal_join self-join check removed → aborted / detected with rollback · M13 deal_create membership check removed → SURVIVES (18/18) because the test's 1-char title 'x' violates the title length CHECK; with a 2-char title the same test fails as intended. Net: 12/14 detected, 2 named assertions vacuous, 4 runs produced no report at all because the harness has no error isolation and psql exits 0.

COVERAGE MATRIX (feature → suite → what is really asserted → gap): A foundation → schema_sweep + parity tool → RLS on/with policy, one counter trigger → no check of anon-executable security-definer RPCs, storage policies, or column-level grants. B auth → b1 → signup/login/logout/dup/wrong pw in DB, bcrypt, own-profile RLS → NO test that a member cannot set profiles.role/verified (it CAN: critical), no anon-read test, no token expiry/refresh, suite is vacuous on 2nd run (fixed email). B feed → b2 → post/like/comment/save/delete anchored in DB, cross-user delete/react refused → no comment edit/delete by others, no admin delete, no XSS in live posts. B network/notifications → b3 → request/accept/decline/notify/suggest in DB, forged request/accept/notification read refused → ok. B messages → b4 → delivery, read receipts, heads, cross-user read/send/edit/fake-receipt refused → ok (only suite with real condition waits). C companies → c1 + company_hub.rls → wizard→pending/free, edits, verification request, self-verify/VIP refused, Arabic search → team invite/consent/routing untested live. C deals → c2 + deals.rls → full quote path, out-of-turn/expired refused, trail, track record → 2 vacuous negatives (findings), no decline/cancel path, no service/dossier/job flows live. C listings/group → c3 + listings_groups.rls → surplus/dossier, group join→target→confirm→orders, AVL, compare → ok. D market → d1 → real cards, contact/quote, publish, foreign listing edit refused → order 'newest first' not asserted, boosted/featured ordering untested. D jobs → d2a → post/apply once/save, cross-candidate read refused → employer reading another employer's applications untested.
