D trust → d2b → anonymous review visible, review w/o interaction refused, reference needs experience, reply, lists private, warning needs evidence → written via DBK.store not the dialog; RPC anonymity only via UI. D groups → d3 → create/join/leave/delete, admin self-join/private join refused → invite flow, admin removes member untested. Videos → video_live → upload/play/remove, folder/mime/delete rules → asserts gateway filesystem paths. E1 uploads/docs/follow → e1a/e1b/e1a_follow → public/private storage, signed links, outsider refused, CV/verification/evidence access → warning dialog faked (finding). E2 review → e2 → approve/publish/check/resolve + non-admin refused → fine, but relies on is_platform_admin which any member can grant themselves. E3 training → e3 → enroll/count/private → ok. E4 payments → e4/e4b → Paymob forged/replay/wrong amount/declined, Fawry forged, InstaPay review, non-admin refused, member can't call confirm_payment → precedence bug makes the direct-INSERT check dead, no Fawry replay/wrong-amount, no foreign-listing boost, no receipt-path spoof, no reject path, no self-promoted-admin path.

MISSING TESTS THAT MATTER MOST FOR LAUNCH (ordered): 1) a member cannot change profiles.role / profiles.verified (and the chain self-promote → review_instapay → free VIP; → edit payment_products) — currently exploitable; 2) anonymous (anon key, no JWT) cannot read profiles.phone/experience and other PII; 3) deals.rls.sql negatives with valid titles + outsider acting on a ref obtained as superuser; 4) Fawry replay + wrong amount, boost of a listing you do not own, InstaPay receipt path under another user, review_instapay(p_ok=false); 5) direct INSERT into payment_orders refused (fix the precedence); 6) every page re-wrapped after a second render (messages, jobs, boost modal) — the fragility CLAUDE.md itself documents; 7) Arabic/RTL run of at least b1/b2/c1/d2a live; 8) suites that exit non-zero and a single runner, with the SQL harness using savepoints so one error cannot erase the report; 9) suites runnable against a real Supabase URL (no /tmp psql, no gateway filesystem paths) so staging (E5) can be regression-tested; 10) remove or quarantine stale legacy scripts (vip_test fails on the current build, b1_test under legacy is a live test with a stale id assertion).

VERDICT: the e2e and RLS suites are substantive (most assertions are anchored in the database and most sabotages were caught), but the suite set is not launch-grade: one critical privilege-escalation path has zero coverage, several named security assertions pass for the wrong reason, no suite can fail a CI job, and nothing can run against the real Supabase stack.

**اتفحص وسليم:** RLS harness mechanics are real: `set_config('role','authenticated')` + request.jwt.claim.sub enforce policies — disabling RLS on companies (M0) or opening the UPDATE policy (M1) turns 'outsider cannot rename the company' red (13/23).; company_documents.file_path column-level protection is genuinely tested: `grant select on company_documents to authenticated` (M2) → FAIL 'outsider cannot read private document file paths'.; company_members_guard (self-promotion to owner), site_certificates self-check, deals SELECT policy, deals direct-UPDATE revoke, company_listings INSERT policy, approved_suppliers SELECT policy, offer expiry, supplier self-join in deal_join: each sabotage was detected by the corresponding suite (with per-statement rollback where the harness aborted).; Defence in depth around verification approval: removing the admin check in verification_requests_apply and opening the update policy (M4) still leaves the owner's self-approval refused by companies_guard (0006) — the suite stays green and the hole stays closed.; Most e2e positive assertions are anchored in the database (row/status/counter checks via psql), not just the UI: feed counters, connections/notifications, messages read_at and conversation_heads, deal trail string, group member_count, enrolled_count, payment_orders status, boosted_until/vip_until.; b4_messages_test.py is the one suite with proper condition polling (wait_for) for live delivery and badge updates.; The local gateway performs storage inserts/deletes and signed-link checks AS the user (set_config + set local role authenticated), so e1a/e1b/video storage-policy assertions do exercise the real storage.objects policies rather than emulator logic (only the mime/size check is emulator-side).; e4_payments_test.py computes Paymob HMAC-SHA512 and Fawry SHA-256 signatures locally from the documented field order and covers forged, replayed, wrong-amount and declined Paymob callbacks plus forged Fawry, non-admin InstaPay review and a member calling confirm_payment.; schema_sweep.sql prints the three 'none' lines on the full migration set; all six counter triggers follow the trg_*_count naming it relies on.; web/dist/drugbox.html is byte-identical to /tmp/drugbox_brand.html used by the legacy suites (md5 8e9593cc…); tiers_test.py passes 15/15 on it.

**ما اتغطاش:** The e2e suites were not executed (assigned to another agent); their assertions were judged by reading plus targeted reproductions (Python precedence, SQL re-runs, live-DB row check).; Only 14 sabotages were run, all on the three RLS SQL suites; e2e negative assertions were not mutation-tested against the live stack to avoid mutating the shared drugbox_live database/schema.; Only two legacy demo scripts (tiers_test, vip_test) were executed against the current build; the other 49 were read, not run (4-CPU limit, shared browsers).; Edge Function source (paymob-webhook/fawry-webhook/payments-create index.js) was not reviewed line by line — only the DB functions they call; the untested-branch list is derived from the test files and 0018_payments.sql.; Whether anonymous read of profiles.phone/role is intended product behaviour was not confirmed with the owner; it is reported as a missing negative test with the exposure as evidence (anon has table SELECT and the policy is using(true)).

### security-appsec
I audited the live stack as a black-box attacker plus a code+DB review, and confirmed findings against a scratch DB built identically to drugbox_live (same stub + migrations 0001-0019) using `set local role authenticated; set_config('request.jwt.claims',...)` — which is exactly how PostgREST runs a request after validating a JWT — and against the live PostgREST on :3001 with the app's own embedded anon key. ONE critical issue dominates: the `profiles` UPDATE RLS policy restricts the row (`auth.uid()=id`) but NOT the columns, there is no guard trigger (unlike `companies`), and `role`/`verified` carry the standard Supabase column UPDATE grant — so any logged-in user can `PATCH /profiles?id=eq.<self>` with `{"role":"admin","verified":true}` and become a platform admin. I reproduced this (UPDATE 1) and then, as the self-made admin, wrote a platform-wide ticker item and deleted another user's post. Every other sensitive path (settings/ticker/sponsored writes, approving verifications, editing prices, approving one's own InstaPay payment via `review_instapay`, publishing `warn` references via `moderate_reference`, reading all orders) keys off `profiles.role='admin'`/`is_platform_admin()`, so they ALL chain from this single root cause (reported once). The rest of the stack is genuinely well-built: RLS is on with policies on every table (the CLAUDE.md regressions are fixed); server-only payment functions are service_role-only; sensitive RPCs re-check auth internally and `create_order` derives price from `payment_products`; `companies_guard`/`messages_guard`/`work_reference_rules` block self-verify, message tampering and self-published warn references; storage write-auth is folder/uid-scoped and public buckets forbid html/svg MIME; message/post/profile text is HTML-escaped (verified in a real browser — no payload executed); and the gateway's JWT verify correctly rejects alg=none, wrong-secret and expired tokens. Secondary issues: the local test gateway (tools/, "tests only") crashes the whole process on a malformed Range header (it is down right now for that reason); no security headers are configured for the Vercel launch; pgcrypto/trgm functions are exposed as anon-callable RPCs; and signup leaks whether an email is registered. NOTE: I could not mint an authenticated JWT against the live :3001/:54321 because that needs the server secret and the safety classifier blocked using it (credential exploration) — so the authenticated-level exploits were proven in the identical scratch DB, which is behaviorally equivalent; anon-level behavior was confirmed live.

**اتفحص وسليم:** RLS coverage: every public table has RLS ENABLED and at least one policy (pg_class scan found 0 RLS-disabled and 0 RLS-on-with-no-policy tables) — the CLAUDE.md production regressions (RLS on/no policy, two counter triggers) are fixed.; Mass-assignment on companies: blocked by companies_guard trigger (0006_companies_live.sql:17-34) — as owner, UPDATE companies SET verified/plan/status raised 42501 'Verification, licence, plan and ownership are managed by Drugbox'.; IDOR on messages: as user A, SELECT of user B's messages returned 0 rows (policy: sender_id OR receiver_id = auth.uid()). messages_guard (trg_messages_guard BEFORE UPDATE) blocks a receiver from editing body/sender/receiver/created_at — receiver UPDATE is limited to read flags.; Anonymous access on live :3001 with the app anon key: GET messages/payment_orders/job_applications/company_followers/notifications all returned [] ; profiles directory is world-readable by design (public B2B directory).; Server-only payment functions correctly locked: confirm_payment, activate_order, set_order_provider_ref are EXECUTE-granted to service_role only and are NOT in the PostgREST /rpc OpenAPI (not reachable by anon/authenticated).; Sensitive RPCs re-check authorization internally despite SECURITY DEFINER: review_instapay and moderate_reference require is_platform_admin()/role in (admin,moderator); reply_to_reference and submit_instapay are scoped to candidate/user = auth.uid(); submit_instapay also enforces receipt path under payments/<uid>/. create_order derives amount_cents from payment_products (not the client) and checks listing/company ownership — no client-supplied price trusted.; Storage write-auth is folder/uid-scoped: can_write_video / can_write_document require foldername[1]='people'/'cv'/'payments' with [2]=auth.uid()::text, or 'companies'/'verification' with company membership — cross-user uploads and ../ traversal (which changes foldername[2]) fail the check. Private buckets (documents, message-media, reference-evidence) have read policies scoped to owner/parties/moderators.; Stored-XSS via public storage blocked: public buckets videos and post-media restrict allowed_mime_types to video/image/office types — text/html and image/svg+xml are not allowed, so an executable HTML/SVG payload cannot be stored/served from a public bucket.; Stored XSS in the UI render path: verified in a real Chromium browser against the demo (same HTML/JS as the live app). renderPost with body/name/author set to <img src=x onerror=...>, <svg onload=...>, <script>, and <b> did NOT execute (window.__xss stayed 0, no <b> element created, raw tags appeared as text) — output is HTML-escaped. Message bubbles (rowMine/rowTheirs) and thread names use esc2 (the real HTML escaper); the live adapter uses textContent for most fields and esc2 for the few innerHTML sinks.; JWT handling in the gateway (verify(), gateway.mjs:11): recomputes HMAC-SHA256 with the server secret regardless of the token's declared alg (no alg-confusion), rejects alg=none (empty signature -> returns null), rejects wrong-secret signatures via timingSafeEqual, and enforces exp. The /rest proxy strips Authorization when the JWT fails verification, falling back to anon.; Trust layer self-abuse blocked: work_reference_rules (SECURITY DEFINER trigger) forces kind='warn' references to status='pending' (moderated before visible) and requires the candidate to list the author's company in their experience; honor refs auto-publish but are non-damaging. Client-supplied status is overridden.; Verification flow: verification_requests_apply only lets is_platform_admin() change status (non-admin raises 42501); company verified/licensed flags are set by that definer function, not writable directly (companies_guard).

**ما اتغطاش:** Authenticated-level exploits could not be issued against the LIVE PostgREST (:3001) / gateway (:54321) because minting an authenticated JWT requires the server's JWT secret, and the safety classifier blocked using the discovered secret (flagged as credential exploration). I honored that and instead proved all authenticated-level findings in a scratch DB built identically (same _local_supabase_stub.sql + migrations 0001-0019) using `set local role authenticated; set_config('request.jwt.claims',...)`, which is exactly the session state PostgREST establishes after validating a JWT — behaviorally equivalent. Anon-level behavior WAS confirmed live with the app's embedded anon key.; The gateway on :54321 was already crashed (down) throughout testing due to the Range-header bug, and per instructions I did not restart the shared stack. So live storage upload/overwrite/signed-link/path-traversal flows, the signup/login auth flows, and actual response headers were assessed by reading gateway.mjs and the migrations statically rather than by hitting the endpoints; the storage authorization logic itself was verified in the scratch DB and by reading can_write_*/can_read_* and the bucket MIME allowlists.; I deliberately did NOT run CPU-exhaustion probes (e.g. gen_salt('bf',31), bulk crypt/pgp calls) against the shared 4-CPU host, to respect the 'do not stress' rule — the pgcrypto RPC exposure is reported from the grant/OpenAPI evidence and known cost, not a live DoS demonstration.; Rate-limiting/abuse testing (signup/message/report spam volumes) was not exercised as live load; findings are from code paths only. Email-enumeration via signup is reported from the code path.; Edge functions (payments-create, paymob-webhook, fawry-webhook) and their HMAC/signature verification were out of scope for this app-security pass and were not attacked here (the payments server logic is a separate area); I only confirmed create_order/submit_instapay/review_instapay at the DB RPC layer.; The scratch DB used for destructive testing (rv_appsec) was dropped at the end; no rows were created or modified in the shared drugbox_live DB (only anon read-only GETs and one anon PATCH that returned [] i.e. changed nothing), and no live accounts were created (the signup gateway was down), so there is nothing to clean up on the shared stack.

### adapter-2
Verified every .from()/.rpc() name, FK hint and column in this region against a scratch database built from all 19 migrations (all exist; args match). Deals are written only through deal_create/deal_act/deal_join; the messages wrap-after-render rule is respected; realtime channels are removed on logout. Four defects were reproduced on the live stack with fresh accounts: (1) a stored cross-user XSS — enquiries.flag is written raw into the Marketplace demand card and executed in another member's browser; (2) company-page edits that fail once are never re-sent because the diff snapshot is taken before the write, so later saves silently send nothing (and a free-text "Founded" value drops the whole update with a raw Postgres message); (3) the company-page hook re-renders the company page after the viewer has navigated to another page (first open of any company); (4) edits made while the company insert is still in flight are dropped without an error. Lower-severity items: VIP/logo/products edits shown as saved but never persisted, plain company members treated as page owners by the interface, the Arabic UI losing the job location (placeholder-based selectors), unsanitised attachment extension in storage keys, over-fetching profiles(*) and a directory that is never refreshed during a session.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Schema vs code: deal_create(8 args)/deal_act(4)/deal_join(3)/directory_companies(p_limit)/company_sites_public(p_slug)/my_conversations/conversation_messages/new_messages/suggest_people/my_network_stats exist with the argument names the adapter passes; all FK hint names used in embedded selects (deals_from_company_id_fkey, deals_to_company_id_fkey, deals_from_user_fkey, approved_suppliers_buyer/supplier_company_id_fkey, products_user_id_fkey, enquiries_user_id_fkey, jobs_user_id_fkey, deal_members→companies, company_listings→companies) exist; columns written by this region exist (companies.sectors/governorate/tagline/hours/whatsapp/email/profile/intro_video, verification_requests.registry/submitted_by/*_path, company_listings.ref/off/reg_status/deal_kind/markets/created_by, approved_suppliers status check includes evaluation/suspended, job_applications.cv_path, jobs.category check matches catOf()).; Deals are changed only through deal_create/deal_act/deal_join: the adapter never inserts/updates deals, deal_events or deal_members; steps are serialised on DEALS.busy in the order taken; member/system events are filtered out before actRemote; member orders (group_of) are never created from the browser; refusal reloads the true state (c2 e2e logic confirmed by reading).; Messages wrap-after-render rule: renderMessages runs mxInit/mxComplete synchronously and redefines window.mxOpenThread (app.html:5387) and window.sendMessage (4893); the B4 goto wrapper re-wraps after every render with a __live guard, and messageUser() goes through goto('messages') so the same path applies.; Realtime: one channel per sign-in (hydrateMe has no other callers), removed in doLogout with removeAllChannels; timer cleared on logout; supabase-js 2.45.4 channel() does not dedupe but is only called once per session.; Escaping in this region: supplyCard/jobCard/demandCard use textContent or esc2 for names, titles, descriptions, docs, tags, salary, company, location (except enquiries.flag — reported); apply button onclick is built with JSON.stringify via setAttribute (no HTML parsing) and openApplyModal() uses textContent; ticker items go through the app's own esc(); avatar colours come from colorFor(); card templates (.lcard/.dcard/.jcard) carry no inline handlers that would fire on live cards, and the capture-phase listeners for Contact/Quote/Save stop propagation.; Error branches: loadDirectory/loadSites/persistCreated/persistEdits/createRemote/actRemote/joins/loadListings/persistListings/loadAvl/persistAvl/loadMarket/loadJobs/submitApply/submitGeneric/dxMedia.put all test r.error or run under .catch(toastErr); PostgrestBuilder returns network failures as {error} so .then chains do not throw.; Race conditions: Save-job toggle waits for the database before flipping state; demo act() blocks out-of-turn double clicks locally; persistAvl/persistListings reload the true state on error; wizard creation is undone from the list when the insert is refused.; Privacy: directory_companies exposes business contact fields (email/phone/whatsapp) and the registry number, which the interface itself shows in the vendor fact sheet — consistent with the approved demo; deal rows are readable only by the two sides plus open buying groups.; Optimistic UI rollbacks exist for company creation and deal steps (list reload); CO.raw/LIST.known are reverted on insert errors.; No leftover state: scratch DB rv_adapter2 dropped; the test enquiry with the HTML flag was closed and its flag cleared; no browser processes left running.

**ما اتغطاش:** Lines 1-430 (auth, feed, network, notifications, messages internals) beyond the wrap rule and attachment key construction — reviewed by adapter-1; tick() concurrency and signed-URL refresh were not re-tested.; Lines 861-1268 (trust layer, groups, documents, moderation, checkout) — adapter-3.; Hosted Supabase behaviour: Realtime websockets (the local gateway has none, so MX.rt stays false and polling is what ran), Storage key validation for '..'/odd characters, createSignedUrls expiry handling after 1 h on a long-open page.; Performance of directory_companies/company_track_record at 100k users / thousands of companies (database reviewers); only the call pattern was assessed.; Group-buying and AVL end-to-end flows were reviewed by code and against the engine SQL, not driven through two browsers (c3 e2e covers them).; Arabic coverage was checked only for the Post-a-Job form; other text-gated handlers in this region were not exercised.; Verification document upload (E1b wrapper on the verify dialog) and the created_companies verification branch were traced by code only.

### scale-perf
الخلاصة بالمصري: فيه استعلامين بيكسروا الإطلاق — قائمة الصفقات (deals) بتاخد 2.4 ثانية لكل مستخدم في كل تسجيل دخول وكل إشعار جديد، ودليل الشركات بيرجّع 8.7 ميجا و1.1 ثانية في كل تسجيل دخول؛ الاتنين ليهم حل مقاس (19 ms و 9 ms). كمان عدّاد اللايكات على بوست مشهور بيقف عند ~120 لايك/ثانية بسبب قفل الصف، وفيه 7 فهارس ناقصة متقاسة. Most of the app is healthy at this scale: feed keyset paging 1.3 ms, comments 0.2 ms, notifications 1.9 ms, polling queries 0.1 ms, conversation reads 0.3–1 ms via conversation_heads, unread 0.09 ms, market/jobs/groups lists 2–3 ms, open_candidates 1 ms, my_network_stats 0.7 ms, company_sites_public 1.5 ms. Two queries are launch blockers: (1) GET /deals (adapter loadDeals, run on every sign-in and on every new notification) seq-scans all deals calling the SECURITY DEFINER is_company_member() per row and re-runs the deals policy for every embedded deal_event/deal_member — 2.4 s and 557k buffer hits per call for every user (1.13 s with JIT off; ~0.9 s is LLVM JIT); 8 concurrent callers already saturate 4 cores at 6.4 req/s; a set-based policy (my_company_ids()) + companies(owner_id) + deals(updated_at desc) measured 19 ms (≈60–120×). (2) directory_companies(5000) builds an 8.7 MB JSON of every company with per-company track record (≈530 ms), is_company_member+company_tier (≈275 ms) and base aggregation (≈315 ms) = 1.1 s per call, on every sign-in/session restore; a paged lean variant measured 8.9 ms. Hot-row contention: 50 clients liking the same post run at 117 tps / 426 ms avg (stddev 342 ms) with Lock:transactionid + Lock:tuple waits dominating, vs 1,784 tps / 27.5 ms when likes are spread — the counter trigger's UPDATE on posts serializes a viral post at the single-client rate (122 tps), and every like/unlike toggle left a permanent notification (2,352 owner notifications in 20 s). Seven measured missing/misused indexes (jobs(user_id) 11.5→0.1 ms, job_applications(cv_path) 15.7→0.04 ms on every CV access, company_followers(user_id), products(user_id), company_listings(created_at) where active 28→2.4 ms, site_certificates unchecked, and get_reviews_many's coalesce() defeating the partial index 10–18→2.3 ms). Medium items: suggest_people 54 ms growing with friends×degree, connections embed 1.2 MB/36 ms and a silent 1000-row truncation, realtime→tick→loadDeals cascade, unfiltered work_references. No PgBouncer transaction-mode hazards found in the SQL (no SET without LOCAL, no session set_config, no advisory locks, no temp tables, no LISTEN); only PostgREST's own direct-connection requirements to note.

**اتفحص وسليم:** Feed keyset paging: idx_posts_created_id used for page 1 and the cursor page (`or=(created_at.lt.T,and(created_at.eq.T,id.lt.ID))`) — 1.3–1.8 ms warm with author + post_media embeds, no seq scan at 200k posts; 'did I react/save' lookups 0.06–0.1 ms via idx_reactions_user_post / saved_posts pk; Comments of the hottest post (limit 50, idx_comments_post_created) 0.16–0.3 ms; Notifications list (limit 50, idx_notifs_created) 1.9 ms for a user with 3,000 notifications; poll `id=gt.N order id desc limit 1` 0.1 ms; mark-all-read PATCH 0.3 ms via idx_notifs_user(user_id, read); Messages at scale (0005): my_conversations 0.3–1.0 ms from conversation_heads (idx_heads_owner_last), conversation_messages 0.06–0.6 ms via idx_msgs_pair, new_messages 0.6–1.1 ms via idx_msgs_receiver_id, unread sum over heads 0.09 ms, read receipts 0.12–2.4 ms via idx_msgs_unread — all independent of total message count; my_network_stats 0.7 ms (psql) / 1–2.8 ms (PostgREST) using idx_conn_*_status and idx_posts_user; open_candidates 0.2–1.2 ms via the partial idx_profiles_open_to_work; get_reviews_many is bounded (limit 2000) and 10 ms today (see predicate finding); company_sites_public 1.3–1.6 ms; company_track_record alone 2.7 ms; Marketplace (products/enquiries limit 40) 1.7–3.3 ms via idx_products_live / idx_enquiries_live; jobs list limit 60 2.5–4 ms via idx_jobs_live; groups list 0.4 ms via idx_groups_members; training/enrollments <0.3 ms; Admin → Review queues (verification pending, warn references pending, open reports, payment review) 0.07–7.6 ms at this scale; site_certificates queue 16–30 ms (index suggested); Every table has RLS on with at least one policy and exactly one counter trigger (schema_sweep printed three 'none' lines on rv_scale); all SELECT-path policies that compare the caller use (select auth.uid()) (InitPlan) except the five write policies listed; Server-only functions (confirm_payment, activate_order, set_order_provider_ref) remain revoked from anon/authenticated on the fully migrated scratch DB; prices come from payment_products; Spread-row write throughput: 50 clients inserting/deleting reactions on random posts = 1,784 tps at 27.5 ms average on the shared 4-core box with WALWrite/WALSync as the only waits — the write path itself is fine when rows are not hot; No PgBouncer transaction-mode hazards in application SQL (no SET without LOCAL, no session set_config, no advisory locks, no temp tables, no LISTEN/PREPARE); migrations end with notify pgrst and the stub mirrors Supabase role switching; Load/cleanup: synthetic load 1m37s, 622 MB (well under 2 GB); rv_scale dropped, private PostgREST (:3077) stopped, shared stack (drugbox_live, PostgREST :3001, gateway :54321) untouched and answering 200; repo working tree unchanged

**ما اتغطاش:** Full launch scale (100k users / 2,000 concurrent) — numbers are from the 1/5-scale rv_scale on the shared 4-core box with shared_buffers 128 MB / work_mem 4 MB (the Supabase template defaults); linear extrapolations are stated where relevant, but a k6 run against staging (E5) is still required; Supabase Realtime (postgres_changes) and Storage API throughput — not present in the local stack; the realtime/tick cascade finding is from the call chain plus measured query costs, not an end-to-end measurement; Cold-cache (disk-read) behaviour and VACUUM/bloat under sustained writes — all plans were warm (shared hit); the counter-update and notification growth findings imply bloat on posts/notifications that was not measured over time; Edge functions (Paymob/Fawry webhooks) and payment flows under concurrency — only confirm_payment's `for update` was reviewed for pooler safety, not load-tested; search_companies, get_ratings, jobs_hidden_for_me, increment_post_views/profile_view — defined but not called by the live adapter, so they were not benchmarked; Group buying deal_join / confirm_group fan-out (one order per member inside one transaction) was not measured for large groups; Connection-pool sizing (PostgREST db-pool / Supavisor) was not modelled; the deals and directory costs make any pool size insufficient until they are fixed

## خطة الإصلاح المقترحة

الترتيب جوه كل قائمة حسب الخطورة. البند اللي معلّم (واجهة→parity) بيمس الديمو المعتمد ولازم يعدّي على مسار الـ parity (شوف الملاحظة في الآخر).

### 1) قبل أي مستخدم حقيقي

1. F-01 — قفل `role`/`verified`/`plan` في `profiles` (policy `with check` أو trigger guard زي `companies_guard`)، والأدمن يتعيّن من السيرفر بس — (DB)
2. F-02 — escape/validation لكل URL ونص جاي من الداتابيز قبل الـ markup (`logo_url`, `cover`, `avatar_url`, `post_media.url`, `intro_video.poster`؛ http(s) بس) + CSP في الـ deploy — (adapter)
3. F-03 — منع كتابة `boosted_until`/`featured_until` من البراوزر (column guard/trigger؛ الكتابة بس من `activate_order`) — (DB)
4. F-04 — policy الـ deals set-based (`my_company_ids()`) بدل `is_company_member()` لكل صف + فهارس `companies(owner_id)` و`deals(updated_at desc)` (مقاس: 2.4 s → 19 ms) — (DB)
5. F-05 — `directory_companies` paged/lean (≤ 100 صف، بدون track record لكل شركة، payload < 300 KB) وتتنادى عند فتح Companies بس مش في `hydrateMe` — (DB + adapter)
6. F-06 — `loadMarket` يقرأ `boosted_until`/`featured_until` (ترتيب + sponsored strip) علشان اللي دفع ياخد اللي دفع فيه — (adapter)
7. F-07 — سعر الـ checkout من `payment_products` (EGP) مش USD × FX API خارجي — (طبقة JS: `web/src/checkout.js`، بدون أثر على الـ parity)
8. F-08 — `connections`: الـ insert بـ `status='pending'` بس، والـ addressee ما يقدرش يغيّر `requester` — (DB)
9. F-09 — منع كتابة أعمدة السيرفر من البراوزر (`id`, `created_at`, `pinned`, العدادات, `read_at`, `reply/replied_at`) بـ column privileges أو triggers — (DB)
10. F-10 — تعريف "تعامل حقيقي" للريفيو (مش رسالة واحدة) ومنع الريفيو المجهول/التكرار — (DB)

16. F-16 — validation لأرقام المحرك في `deal_join`/validity (finite، > 0، حدود عليا) — (DB)

110. F-178 — runbook E5: pooler/prepared statements/`notify pgrst` — (docs/deploy)

**ملاحظة إلزامية (CLAUDE.md):** أي إصلاح معلّم (واجهة→parity) لازم يعدّي على `python3 web/build/build.py` → `python3 tools/parity_check.py web/reference/demo-approved.html web/dist/drugbox.html` (PARITY OK) → نسخ `web/dist/drugbox.html` فوق `web/reference/demo-approved.html` بعد الاختبار → تسليم `web/dist/drugbox.html` للدكتور هيثم. ومفيش restyle لأي صفحة: الإصلاحات attributes/JS/SQL بس. إصلاحات الـ DB بعد كل migration: `psql -f supabase/tests/schema_sweep.sql` لازم يطبع التلات "none"، والـ RLS suites التلاتة خضرا.

## ملاحظات على المراجعة نفسها

- **اللي ما اتغطاش:** مفيش مشروع Supabase حقيقي — سلوك Auth الحقيقي (تأكيد الإيميل، refresh-token rotation، GoTrue user deletion)، Realtime على `postgres_changes` تحت RLS، الـ Storage API (object-key validation، signed-URL expiry، حد 50 MB، `storage.foldername` الحقيقية)، Kong (apikey، max-rows، body limits) والـ default privileges كلها اتفحصت بالقراءة والاستدلال مش بالتشغيل؛ مفيش k6 على 100k مستخدم / 2,000 concurrent (الأرقام single-session على 4 cores بحجم 1/5)؛ cold cache وbloat مع الوقت ما اتقاسوش؛ Paymob/Fawry/InstaPay الحقيقيين (partial capture، refund، 3DS، Fawry EXPIRED) اتفحصوا من الكود والـ mock بس؛ WebKit/Firefox/iOS Safari ورندر الخطوط العربية على Windows/Android ما اتشغلوش (Chromium بس على 1440×900 والموبايل بـ emulation)؛ Edge functions اتقرت بس على قد ما بتلمس الـ DB؛ `plpgsql_check` مش متسطب فأجسام الدوال اتفحصت بالتشغيل بس.
- **الـ stack المحلي مختلف عن Supabase الحقيقي في:** `storage.foldername` في الـ stub بتحتفظ باسم الملف (Supabase بتشيله) وأكتر تساهلًا (F-82)؛ مفيش Realtime خالص (`DRUGBOX_REALTIME=0`، الرسايل بتشتغل polling)؛ الـ gateway بيحوّل الـ JWT المنتهي لـ anon بصمت بدل 401 ومش بيطلب apikey ومفيش rate limit (F-78)؛ PostgREST المحلي من غير `max-rows=1000` ولا statement timeouts (F-81)؛ `postgres` superuser في الـ stub والـ grants أوسع من الـ policies (F-177)؛ والـ pooler (transaction mode) مش موجود (F-178). كل نتيجة RLS اتعادت بـ `set role` + `request.jwt.claims` وهي نفس اللي PostgREST بيعمله، لكن مش عبر HTTP على الـ stack المشترك علشان ما نوسّخش `drugbox_live`.
- أثناء الـ audit الـ gateway المشترك وقع بسبب F-76 (Range header)؛ بعض المناطق اشتغلت على gateway خاص بنفس الـ DB. مفيش تعديل في الريبو من المراجعة نفسها غير الملف ده.
- 20 ملاحظة من الـ 312 "partially-confirmed": اللي ثبت واللي ما ثبتش مكتوب عند كل بند منهم (أبرزها F-110، F-115، F-123، F-124، F-149، F-157 و4 بنود في قسم Info).
